\displaystyle+\Big|\Big(\nabla_{H}\int_{z}^{0}\Big[v_{s}\boldsymbol{\cdot}\nabla_{H}w_{s}+w_{s}\partial_{z}w_{s}-\Big[\frac{1}{2}\nabla\boldsymbol{\cdot}\hat{a}[\nabla v_{s}]+\frac{1}{2}\nabla\boldsymbol{\cdot}\hat{\hat{a}}[\nabla w_{s}]\Big]\Big]dz^{\prime},v_{n}^{*}\Big)_{L^{2}}\Big|

\displaystyle+\Big|\Big(\nabla_{H}\int_{z}^{0}\Big[\frac{1}{2}\sum_{k}\phi_{k}\boldsymbol{\cdot}\nabla\partial_{z}\pi_{k}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{k}(\phi_{k}\boldsymbol{\cdot}\nabla)\Delta\phi_{k}^{z}dz^{\prime}\Big],v_{n}^{*}\Big)_{L^{2}}\Big|

\displaystyle\leq\cancel{\Big|\Big(v_{s}\boldsymbol{\cdot}\nabla_{H}w(v_{n}^{*})+w_{s}\partial_{z}w(v_{n}^{*}),w(v_{n}^{*})\Big)_{L^{2}}\Big|}+\Big(\frac{1}{2}\Big[\nabla\boldsymbol{\cdot}\hat{a}[\nabla v_{n}^{*}]+\nabla\boldsymbol{\cdot}\hat{\hat{a}}[\nabla w(v_{n}^{*})]\Big],w(v_{n}^{*})\Big)_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle-(\alpha(-\Delta)^{\gamma_{r}}w(v_{n}^{*}),w(v_{n}^{*})\Big)_{L^{2}}+C\|U_{n}^{*}\|_{H}\|U_{n}^{*}\|_{V}+C\|U_{n}^{*}\|_{V}+\frac{1}{2}\Big|\Big(\sum_{k}\phi_{k}\boldsymbol{\cdot}\nabla\partial_{z}\pi_{k},w(v_{n}^{*})\Big)_{L^{2}}\Big|

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{1}{2}\Big(\hat{a}[\nabla v_{n}^{*}]+\hat{\hat{a}}[\nabla w(v_{n}^{*})],\nabla_{3}w(v_{n}^{*})\Big)_{L^{2}}-\alpha\|w(v_{n}^{*})\|_{H^{\gamma_{r}}}^{2}+C\|U_{n}^{*}\|_{H}\|U_{n}^{*}\|_{V}+C\|U_{n}^{*}\|_{V}

\displaystyle+C\Big(\sum_{k}\|\phi_{k}\|_{L^{\infty}}\Big)\underbrace{\|\nabla w(v_{n}^{*})\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{z}\pi_{k}\|_{L^{2}}}_{\leq C(1+\|w(v_{n}^{*})\|_{H^{1}}^{2})},

where we used that
\phi_{k}
is divergence-free, and that
\partial_{z}\pi_{k}=-\phi_{k}\boldsymbol{\cdot}\nabla w+\Delta\phi_{k}^{z}
. Then, we may conclude by Sobolev interpolation and Young’s inequality.

First term of the total covariation noise contribution

\displaystyle\Big\|\Big[G_{\sigma}^{n}(U_{n}^{*})-

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}^{\sigma,U_{n}^{*}}\Big]^{*}U_{n}^{*}\Big\|_{H}^{2}=\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\Big[\cancel{(w(v_{n}^{*}),\phi_{k}\boldsymbol{\cdot}\nabla_{3}w(v_{n}^{*}))_{L^{2}}}+(w(v_{n}^{*}),\phi_{k}\boldsymbol{\cdot}\nabla_{3}w_{s})_{L^{2}}\Big]

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\Big[(w(v_{n}^{*}),A\phi_{k}^{z})_{L^{2}}-\cancel{(v_{n}^{*},[\phi_{k}\boldsymbol{\cdot}\nabla_{3}v_{n}^{*}])_{L^{2}}}-(v_{n}^{*},\phi_{k}\boldsymbol{\cdot}\nabla_{3}v_{s}+A\phi_{k}^{H}+\Gamma\phi_{k}^{H})_{L^{2}}\Big]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}C\|\phi_{k}\|_{H^{3}}^{2}(\|v_{n}^{*}\|_{H}^{2}+\|w(v_{n}^{*})\|_{L^{2}}^{2})\leq C(\|U_{n}^{*}\|_{H}^{2}+\|w(v_{n}^{*})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}).

Second term of the total covariation noise contribution

\frac{1}{2}\Big\|G_{\sigma}^{n}(U_{n}^{*})-\mathcal{M}^{\sigma,U_{n}^{*}}\Big\|_{\mathcal{L}_{2}(\mathcal{W},H)}^{2}=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\Big\|\mathbf{P}[\phi_{k}\boldsymbol{\cdot}\nabla_{3}v_{n}^{*}+\phi_{k}\boldsymbol{\cdot}\nabla_{3}v_{s}]+A\phi_{k}^{H}+\Gamma\phi_{k}^{H}\Big\|_{H}^{2}\\
+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\Big\|\mathbf{P}\nabla_{H}\pi_{k}\Big\|_{H}^{2}+\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}(\mathbf{P}[\phi_{k}\boldsymbol{\cdot}\nabla_{3}(v_{n}^{*}+v_{s})]+A\phi_{k}^{H}+\Gamma\phi_{k}^{H},\mathbf{P}\nabla_{H}\pi_{k})_{L^{2}}\\
\leq\frac{1}{2}(a_{3}\nabla_{3}v_{n}^{*},\nabla_{3}v_{n}^{*})_{L^{2}}+C_{\xi}(1+\|U_{n}^{*}\|_{H}^{2})+\xi(\|U_{n}^{*}\|_{V}^{2}+\|w(v_{n}^{*})\|_{H^{\gamma_{r}}}^{2})\\
+\Big(\hat{a}[\nabla_{3}v_{n}^{*}]+\frac{1}{2}\hat{\hat{a}}[\nabla_{3}w(v_{n}^{*})],\nabla_{3}w(v_{n}^{*})\Big)_{L^{2}}.

Using those inequalities, we conclude by similar arguments as in subsection
4.2
(Step 3). Notice that the “new” covariation compensation terms lead to the following
w
-dependent energy term
(\hat{a}[\nabla_{3}v_{n}^{*}]+\frac{1}{2}\hat{\hat{a}}[\nabla_{3}w(v_{n}^{*})],\nabla_{3}w(v_{n}^{*}))_{L^{2}},
arising from the computation of the bounded variation pressure associated energy
(U_{n}^{*},\frac{1}{\rho_{0}}\mathbf{P}\nabla_{H}p)_{H}
. As expected, it balances exactly the quadratic covaration term arising from the martingale component
\frac{1}{2}\|G_{\sigma}^{n}(U_{n}^{*})-\mathcal{M}^{\sigma,U_{n}^{*}}\|_{\mathcal{L}_{2}(\mathcal{W},H)}^{2}
. Furthermore, the term
\xi\|w(v_{n}^{*})\|_{H^{\gamma_{r}}}^{2}
is absorbed by the additional viscosity term
\alpha\|w(v_{n}^{*})\|_{H^{\gamma_{r}}}^{2}\geq\xi\|w(v_{n}^{*})\|_{H^{1}}^{2}
. In particular, for all
T>0
, there exists a constant
C
such that, for all
n\in\mathbbm{N}
,

\int_{0}^{T}\|w(v_{n}^{*})\|_{H^{\gamma_{r}}}^{2}\leq C.

\square

B.2
Energy estimate for the problem
(\mathcal{P}^{\gamma_{r}}_{EV})
(Theorem
4
)

Assume that
\gamma_{r}>2
. For the energy estimates, we only need to check for the bounded variation pressure and noise terms. Furthermore, notice that

\displaystyle\Big\|\mathbf{P}\nabla_{H}\pi_{k}\Big\|_{H}^{2}\leq C(1+\|w(v_{n}^{*})\|_{H^{2}}^{2})\leq C_{\xi}(1+\|U_{n}^{*}\|_{H}^{2})+\xi\|w(v_{n}^{*})\|_{H^{\gamma_{r}}}^{2}.

Bounded variation pressure term

\displaystyle\Big|\Big(U_{n}^{*},\frac{1}{\rho_{0}}\mathbf{P}\nabla_{H}p\Big)_{H}\Big|

\displaystyle\leq\Big|\Big(g\>\nabla_{H}\int_{z}^{0}{(\beta_{T}T_{n}+\beta_{S}S_{n})dz^{\prime}},v_{n}^{*}\Big)_{L^{2}}\Big|

\displaystyle+\Big|\Big(\nabla_{H}\int_{z}^{0}\Big[(v_{s}\boldsymbol{\cdot}\nabla_{H}+w_{s}\partial_{z})w(v_{n}^{*})\Big]dz^{\prime},v_{n}^{*}\Big)_{L^{2}}\Big|

\displaystyle+\Big(\nabla_{H}\int_{z}^{0}\Big[\frac{1}{2}\nabla\boldsymbol{\cdot}(a\nabla v_{n}^{*})-\alpha(-\Delta)^{\gamma_{r}}w(v_{n}^{*})\Big]dz^{\prime},v_{n}^{*}\Big)_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\Big|\Big(\nabla_{H}\int_{z}^{0}\Big[(v_{s}\boldsymbol{\cdot}\nabla_{H}+w_{s}\partial_{z})w_{s}-\frac{1}{2}\nabla\boldsymbol{\cdot}(a\nabla v_{s})\Big]dz^{\prime},v_{n}^{*}\Big)_{L^{2}}\Big|

\displaystyle\leq\cancel{\Big|\Big(v_{s}\boldsymbol{\cdot}\nabla_{H}w(v_{n}^{*})+w_{s}\partial_{z}w(v_{n}^{*}),w(v_{n}^{*})\Big)_{L^{2}}\Big|}+\Big(\frac{1}{2}\nabla\boldsymbol{\cdot}(a\nabla v_{n}^{*}),w(v_{n}^{*})\Big)_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle-\Big(\alpha(-\Delta)^{\gamma_{r}}w(v_{n}^{*}),w(v_{n}^{*})\Big)_{L^{2}}+C(\|U_{n}^{*}\|_{H}+1)\|U_{n}^{*}\|_{V}

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\frac{1}{2}\Big|\Big(a\nabla v_{n}^{*},\nabla w(v_{n}^{*})\Big)_{L^{2}}\Big|}_{\leq C\|U_{n}^{*}\|_{H}\|w(v_{n}^{*})\|_{H^{2}}}-\alpha\|w(v_{n}^{*})\|_{H^{\gamma_{r}}}^{2}+C_{\xi}(\|U_{n}^{*}\|_{H}^{2}+1)+\xi\|U_{n}^{*}\|_{V}^{2}.

First term of the total covariation noise contribution

\displaystyle\Big\|\Big[G_{\sigma}^{n}(U_{n}^{*})-

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}^{\sigma,U_{n}^{*}}\Big]^{*}U_{n}^{*}\Big\|_{H}^{2}=\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\Big[\cancel{(w(v_{n}^{*}),\phi_{k}\boldsymbol{\cdot}\nabla_{3}w(v_{n}^{*}))_{L^{2}}}+(w(v_{n}^{*}),\phi_{k}\boldsymbol{\cdot}\nabla_{3}w_{s})_{L^{2}}\Big]

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\Big[(w(v_{n}^{*}),A\phi_{k}^{z})_{L^{2}}-\cancel{(v_{n}^{*},[\phi_{k}\boldsymbol{\cdot}\nabla_{3}v_{n}^{*}])_{L^{2}}}-(v_{n}^{*},\phi_{k}\boldsymbol{\cdot}\nabla_{3}v_{s}+A\phi_{k}^{H}+\Gamma\phi_{k}^{H})_{L^{2}}\Big]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}C\|\phi_{k}\|_{H^{3}}^{2}\|v_{n}^{*}\|_{H}^{2}\leq C\|U_{n}^{*}\|_{H}^{2}.

Second term of the total covariation noise contribution

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\Big\|G_{\sigma}^{n}(U_{n}^{*})-\mathcal{M}^{\sigma,U_{n}^{*}}\Big\|_{\mathcal{L}_{2}(\mathcal{W},H)}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\Big\|\mathbf{P}[\phi_{k}\boldsymbol{\cdot}\nabla_{3}v_{n}^{*}+\phi_{k}\boldsymbol{\cdot}\nabla_{3}v_{s}]+A\phi_{k}^{H}+\Gamma\phi_{k}^{H}\Big\|_{H}^{2}+\Big\|\mathbf{P}\nabla_{H}\pi_{k}\Big\|_{H}^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}(\phi_{k}\boldsymbol{\cdot}\nabla_{3}(v_{n}^{*}+v_{s})+A\phi_{k}^{H}+\Gamma\phi_{k}^{H},\mathbf{P}\nabla_{H}\pi_{k})_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}(a_{3}\nabla_{3}v_{n}^{*},\nabla_{3}v_{n}^{*})_{L^{2}}+

\displaystyle C_{\xi}(1+\|U_{n}^{*}\|_{H}^{2})+\xi(\|U_{n}^{*}\|_{V}^{2}+\|w(v_{n}^{*})\|_{H^{\gamma_{r}}}^{2})+\underbrace{\frac{1}{2}\Big(a\nabla v_{n}^{*},\nabla w(v_{n}^{*})\Big)_{L^{2}}}_{\leq C\|U_{n}^{*}\|_{H}\|w(v_{n}^{*})\|_{H^{2}}}.

Again, we conclude by using similar arguments as in subsection
4.2
(Step 3).

\square
